# Stage 06
## Blocks
| Block | Guide section | Learning points / contexts | Cards | ID range |
|---|---|---|---:|---|
| C2a | 6.7, 5 | Aspect/Aktionsart: delimitative, ingressive, resultative and distributive readings where lexically real; duration, beginning, extent and result contrasts. P; prior aspect packages and prefix semantics. Context, partner and register checked for actual use. | 100 | ru-06-0001–ru-06-0100 |
| C2b | 6.7, 5 | Aspect under negation/modality, intended versus achieved outcomes, repeated bounded action and disputed completion. Narrative/argument with explicit timeline and scope. P; C1 qualification and B2 aspect. | 100 | ru-06-0101–ru-06-0200 |
| C2c | 6.7, 5 | Precise case government: lexical alternation, meaning-dependent complements, prepositional scope and quantity relations. Intellectual, social and professional disputes; governor visible. P; B1 government and advanced collocations. | 100 | ru-06-0201–ru-06-0300 |
| C2d | 6.7, 5 | Motion in complex route/transport and figurative development, action boundaries and lexicalized prefix contrasts. Narrative, transition, negotiation and information flow. P; B2 literal/figurative motion; route/sense explicit. | 100 | ru-06-0301–ru-06-0400 |
| C2e | 6.7, 9, 13 | Participial/adverbial syntax precision, restrictive scope, tense relation and controller repair. Formal/literary excerpts and editing; keep participant and chronology. P/R genre labeled; B2 participial systems and C1 editing. | 100 | ru-06-0401–ru-06-0500 |
| C2f | 6.7 | Fine collocations, semantic prosody and near-synonym selection. Argument, narrative, emotion and professional evaluation; distinguish real senses and registers. P; C1 lexicon, no dictionary-synonym substitution grid. | 100 | ru-06-0501–ru-06-0600 |
| C2g | 6.7, 10 | Word order/focus, contrastive scope, presupposition and rhetorical emphasis. Corrections, objections and restrained praise; contrast or question visible. P; B1/B2 focus and C1 stance. | 100 | ru-06-0601–ru-06-0700 |
| C2h | 6.7, 8 | Discourse particles, understated response, assent/dissent and stance combinations. Short situated exchanges; interlocutor expectation explicit. P; familiar particles and C1 implication. | 100 | ru-06-0701–ru-06-0800 |
| C2i | 6.7, 9 | Formal written style and formal speeches: concession, rhetorical progression, public commitment and measured evaluation. Short invented non-attributed address/report fragments; authority and claim scope visible. P; C1 formal argument. | 100 | ru-06-0801–ru-06-0900 |
| C2j | 6.7, 8 | Colloquial reductions, interruptions, unfinished clauses and rapid group interaction components. Complete recoverable short utterance or two-turn exchange; hidden compact particle/repair. P standard colloquial, R nonstandard forms labeled; not a fragment without interpretable proposition. | 100 | ru-06-0901–ru-06-1000 |
| C2k | 6.7, 7 | Pronunciation/prosody in stance: phrase stress, pause/intonation, consonant/vowel distinctions, stress shifts in demanding inflections. Target/sentence scope explicit; implication supported by words. P/R textual support only; prior sound and discourse systems. | 100 | ru-06-1001–ru-06-1100 |
| C2l | 6.7, 12 | Idioms, conventional metaphors and figurative evaluation, distinguishing frequent neutral, colloquial and marked literary uses. Brief sufficient semantic cue. P/R labeled; B2 idioms and advanced lexical precision. | 100 | ru-06-1101–ru-06-1200 |
| C2m | 6.7, 12 | Humor and irony, understatement and expectation reversal. Explicit situational incongruity, compact hidden stance/collocation. P/R social scope; C1 implication. No unexplained private joke or fabricated real quotation. | 100 | ru-06-1201–ru-06-1300 |
| C2n | 6.7, 12 | Euphemism, indirect disagreement, tact, avoidance and emotional charged speech. Refusal, disappointment, boundaries and sympathy; relationship and intended force visible. P; C1 tact and register. | 100 | ru-06-1301–ru-06-1400 |
| C2o | 6.7, 9 | Literary prose components: narrator stance, image, temporal viewpoint and marked syntax. Original compact passages, literary recognition labeled; context supplies actual interpretation. R/P by objective; participial/narrative prerequisites. | 100 | ru-06-1401–ru-06-1500 |
| C2p | 6.7, 12 | Cultural/historical allusions with interpretable compact evidence, conventional reference and rhetorical function. R; factual allusion must be verified before use, no invented history. Prerequisite literary register and source attribution; cards do not certify cultural literacy. | 100 | ru-06-1501–ru-06-1600 |
| C2q | 6.7, 7, 12 | Regional and generational usage: attributed vocabulary, politeness, colloquial style and standard equivalents. R distinctions; standard Russian production maintained, no generalizations about whole groups. Prerequisite C1 variation; focus on verifiable lexical/social differences. | 100 | ru-06-1601–ru-06-1700 |
| C2r | 6.7, 11 | Unfamiliar professional topics: requesting clarification, defining terms, analogies, conditional advice and responsibility limits. Research, administration, services and technical explanation with cues for new terminology. P; C1 meeting/source language; not hidden entire technical explanations. | 100 | ru-06-1701–ru-06-1800 |
| C2s | 6.7, 13 | Self-editing for rhetorical effect, concision, clarity, force and register; recurring high-risk errors in stress, government, aspect, motion and reference. Generic recurring-error patterns, not claims about this learner. P; earlier systems, visible context preserves meaning. | 100 | ru-06-1801–ru-06-1900 |
| C2t | 6.7, 8, 12–13 | Mediation and objection handling across formal/ordinary registers: preserve attribution, uncertainty, face and commitment while clarifying. P; whole preceding curriculum. Ordinary community and social problems balance literary/official language; each hidden target must add a distinct useful use. | 100 | ru-06-1901–ru-06-2000 |
## Cards
| ID | Block | Hidden learning point |
|---|---|---|
| ru-06-0001 | C2a | played for a while; Past plural, agreeing with дети. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. The activity base is играть; future поиграют. Here the activity lasted for a limited time. Немного and the return home frame the play as a bounded activity. |
| ru-06-0002 | C2a | sat for a while; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Лена. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. The activity base is сидеть; future посидит. The verb presents sitting as limited in duration. |
| ru-06-0003 | C2a | wandered for a while; Past plural, agreeing with мы. Perfective delimitative based on multidirectional бродить, with no simple imperfective partner for the bounded activity; future побродят. По takes the dative for the area. The wandering is on foot here, without a single specified route. |
| ru-06-0004 | C2a | boil for a while; Third-person singular perfective future, used with пусть to give an instruction. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. The activity base is кипеть; future покипят. The instruction calls for a bounded period of boiling. |
| ru-06-0005 | C2a | rested; Past plural, agreeing with мы. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner отдыхать; future отдохнут. Intransitive here. Немного marks a limited rest before the climb continues. |
| ru-06-0006 | C2a | was silent for a while; Masculine singular past, agreeing with Дима. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. The state base is молчать; future помолчит. The silence lasts for a bounded interval before the answer. |
| ru-06-0007 | C2a | dozed for a while; Feminine singular past, agreeing with бабушка. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. The activity base is дремать; future подремлет. Полчаса measures the bounded period of dozing. |
| ru-06-0008 | C2a | swam for a while; Past plural, agreeing with мы. Perfective delimitative based on multidirectional плавать, with no simple imperfective partner for the bounded activity; future поплавают. Here it describes swimming in a pool without a single specified route. |
| ru-06-0009 | C2a | applauded for a while; Past plural, agreeing with зрители. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. The activity base is хлопать; future похлопают. Here it describes applause over a bounded interval. |
| ru-06-0010 | C2a | thought for a while; Feminine singular past; visible «решила» identifies the same speaker as feminine. Perfective; думать is the contextual imperfective activity base; future подумает. Над takes the instrumental for the matter considered. Пару дней measures the period of thought. |
| ru-06-0011 | C2a | started knocking; Past plural, agreeing with ставни. Perfective ingressive; стучать describes the corresponding activity; future застучат. О takes the accusative for the surface struck. The form marks the onset of repeated knocking. |
| ru-06-0012 | C2a | started barking; Feminine singular past, agreeing with собака. Perfective ingressive; лаять describes the corresponding activity; future залает. Intransitive here. The form marks the beginning of barking. |
| ru-06-0013 | C2a | started playing; Masculine singular past, agreeing with оркестр. Perfective ingressive; играть describes the corresponding musical activity; future заиграет. Used without an object here. The form marks the start of the music. |
| ru-06-0014 | C2a | started crying; Masculine singular past, agreeing with малыш. Perfective ingressive; плакать describes the corresponding activity; future заплачет. Intransitive. The form marks the onset of crying after waking. |
| ru-06-0015 | C2a | fell silent; Past plural, agreeing with дети. Perfective ingressive; молчать describes the resulting state; future замолчат. Intransitive. The form marks the transition into silence. |
| ru-06-0016 | C2a | started singing; Past plural, agreeing with сверчки. Perfective ingressive; петь describes the corresponding activity; future запоют. Used without an object here. The form marks the onset of the crickets' sound. |
| ru-06-0017 | C2a | started humming; Masculine singular past, agreeing with проектор. Perfective ingressive; гудеть describes the corresponding sound; future загудит. Intransitive. The form marks the onset of the hum. |
| ru-06-0018 | C2a | started trembling; Past plural, agreeing with руки. Perfective ingressive; дрожать describes the corresponding activity; future задрожат. Intransitive. The form marks the beginning of trembling. |
| ru-06-0019 | C2a | burst out laughing; Past plural, agreeing with все. Perfective ingressive; хохотать describes the corresponding activity; future захохочут. Intransitive. The form marks the onset of loud laughter. |
| ru-06-0020 | C2a | started chirping; Past plural, agreeing with птицы. Perfective ingressive; щебетать describes the corresponding activity; future защебечут. Intransitive. The form marks the onset of chirping. |
| ru-06-0021 | C2a | read through; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Анна. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner перечитывать; future перечитает. Takes an accusative object. Here the action extends through all the letters. With все письма, перечитать presents the reading as covering the whole set. |
| ru-06-0022 | C2a | washed; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Оля. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner перемывать; future перемоет. Takes an accusative object. Here the action covers all the dishes. Всю посуду makes the coverage of the set explicit; the verb does not imply that the dishes were washed again. |
| ru-06-0023 | C2a | went through; Past plural, agreeing with мы. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner перебирать; future переберут. Takes an accusative object. The search goes through the whole set of boxes. Перебрать коробки here means to go through them; it does not guarantee finding the keys. |
| ru-06-0024 | C2a | called each; Masculine singular past, agreeing with Игорь. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner обзванивать; future обзвонит. Takes an accusative person or group. The action is distributed over the participants. |
| ru-06-0025 | C2a | fell; Past plural, agreeing with яблоки. Perfective distributive; падать is the activity base, with no simple imperfective partner for this collective occurrence; future попадают. Intransitive. The form presents multiple objects falling. Here попадать is the distributive verb from падать, distinct from imperfective попадать meaning 'get into' or 'hit'. |
| ru-06-0026 | C2a | sat down in separate places; Reflexive past plural, agreeing with ученики. Perfective; imperfective partner рассаживаться; future рассядутся. По takes the dative for the places occupied. The verb presents the group as settling in separate places. Рассесться по местам describes members of a group taking their respective places. |
| ru-06-0027 | C2a | sent out; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Лена. Perfective; imperfective partner рассылать; future разошлёт. Takes an accusative item and a dative recipient. The invitations are distributed to the stated recipients. |
| ru-06-0028 | C2a | cooked a large quantity; Feminine singular past, agreeing with бабушка. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner наваривать; future наварит. Here it takes a genitive quantity of the food prepared. The prefix presents an accumulated quantity. На всю зиму gives the quantity a practical scale. |
| ru-06-0029 | C2a | gathered a quantity; Past plural, agreeing with мы. Perfective; no simple imperfective partner in this accumulated-quantity sense. The activity base is «собирать». Future «насобирают». It takes a genitive quantity and presents the gathering as yielding an accumulation. |
| ru-06-0030 | C2a | saved up; Past plural, agreeing with дети. Perfective; imperfective partner накапливать; future накопят. It takes an object or quantity expression. The form presents the accumulated amount as achieved within the month. |
| ru-06-0031 | C2a | washed away; Feminine singular past, agreeing with река. Perfective; imperfective partner размывать; future размоет. Takes an accusative object. The completed erosion explains the difficulty of passing. |
| ru-06-0032 | C2a | dried; Neuter singular past, agreeing with солнце. Perfective; imperfective partner высушивать; future высушит. Takes an accusative object. The form presents drying as bringing the path to a dry state. |
| ru-06-0033 | C2a | rose; Neuter singular reflexive past, agreeing with тесто. Perfective; imperfective partner подниматься; future поднимется. Intransitive. Here the dough reaches a risen state over the night. With тесто, подняться means that dough rises; it does not describe travel. |
| ru-06-0034 | C2a | dried up; Feminine singular past, agreeing with лужа. Perfective «высохнуть» pairs with imperfective «высыхать»; future «высохнет». Intransitive. The water in the puddle is gone by morning; the specific mechanism is not stated. |
| ru-06-0035 | C2a | cooled down; Masculine singular past, agreeing with суп. Perfective; imperfective partner остывать; future остынет. Intransitive. За час measures the time taken to reach the cool state. |
| ru-06-0036 | C2a | healed; Feminine singular past, agreeing with рана; stress falls on the ending. Perfective; imperfective partner заживать; future заживёт. Intransitive in this sense. The form presents the wound as having healed. |
| ru-06-0037 | C2a | finished watching; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Нина. Perfective; imperfective partner досматривать; future досмотрит. Takes an accusative object. The form presents the remaining part as watched through to the end. Досмотреть фильм means to finish watching a film already begun; the relative clause supplies the earlier interruption. |
| ru-06-0038 | C2a | finished assembling; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Маша; stress falls on the ending. Perfective; imperfective partner дособирать; future дособерёт. Takes an accusative object. The form presents the remaining assembly as completed. Дособрать пазл means to finish assembling a puzzle that was already partly assembled. |
| ru-06-0039 | C2a | finished eating; Masculine singular past, agreeing with Дима. Perfective; imperfective partner доедать; future доест. Takes an accusative object. The form presents the remaining food as eaten. |
| ru-06-0040 | C2a | wiped; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Маша. Perfective; imperfective partner вытирать; future вытрет. Takes an accusative object. Насухо makes the resulting dry state explicit. |
| ru-06-0041 | C2a | read for a while; Masculine singular past, agreeing with Антон. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. The activity base is читать; future почитает. Used without an object here, it presents reading as bounded by полчаса. |
| ru-06-0042 | C2a | went for a walk; Plural past, agreeing with пассажиры. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. The activity base is гулять; future погуляют. По takes the dative for the area. The walk is a bounded outing, on foot without a single specified route. |
| ru-06-0043 | C2a | lay for a while; Feminine singular past, agreeing with мама. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. The state base is «лежать»; future «полежит». It is intransitive and presents a limited period of lying, here ten minutes, rather than the action of lying down. |
| ru-06-0044 | C2a | stand for a while; Third-person singular future, agreeing with краска. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. The state base is стоять; future постоят. Used intransitively with пусть, it instructs that the paint be left standing for a limited time. |
| ru-06-0045 | C2a | killed by freezing; Masculine singular past, agreeing with «мороз». Perfective; imperfective «перемораживать». Compare future «переморозит». The accusative names the affected objects; here the prefix presents all the flowers as killed by frost. P: The distributive result applies to the whole visible set of flowers. |
| ru-06-0046 | C2a | hesitated for a while; Feminine singular reflexive past, agreeing with Ирина. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. The activity base is колебаться; future поколеблется. Used intransitively here, it presents hesitation as lasting a few seconds. |
| ru-06-0047 | C2a | slept for a while; Feminine singular past, agreeing with малышка. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. The activity base is спать; future поспит. It is intransitive and presents the sleep as a bounded hour. |
| ru-06-0048 | C2a | rehearsed for a while; Plural past, agreeing with музыканты. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. The activity base is репетировать; future порепетируют. Used without an object here, it presents rehearsal as a bounded activity before the break. |
| ru-06-0049 | C2a | made noise for a while; Plural past, agreeing with ребята. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. The activity base is шуметь; future пошумят. It is intransitive and presents the noise as a bounded episode. |
| ru-06-0050 | C2a | warmed herself for a while; Feminine singular reflexive past, agreeing with Вера. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. The activity base is греться; future погреется. Used intransitively, it presents warming oneself as a bounded activity. |
| ru-06-0051 | C2a | began flashing; Feminine singular past, agreeing with молния. Perfective ingressive; сверкать describes the corresponding activity; future засверкает. It is intransitive and marks the onset of flashing. |
| ru-06-0052 | C2a | started whimpering; Masculine singular past, agreeing with ребёнок. Perfective ingressive; хныкать describes the corresponding activity; future захнычет. It is intransitive and marks the onset of whimpering. |
| ru-06-0053 | C2a | began wailing; Feminine singular past, agreeing with сигнализация. Perfective ingressive; выть describes the corresponding sound; future завоет. It is intransitive and marks the beginning of the alarm's wailing sound. |
| ru-06-0054 | C2a | began pattering; Masculine singular past, agreeing with дождь. Perfective ingressive; шуметь describes the corresponding sound; future зашумит. It is intransitive here and marks the beginning of the rain's noise. |
| ru-06-0055 | C2a | began to sway; Feminine singular reflexive past, agreeing with занавеска. Perfective ingressive; колыхаться describes the corresponding motion; future заколышется. It is intransitive and marks the onset of swaying. |
| ru-06-0056 | C2a | began speaking; Plural past, agreeing with ученики. Perfective ingressive; говорить describes the corresponding activity; future заговорят. Used without an object here, it marks the onset of speech. |
| ru-06-0057 | C2a | turned yellow; Plural past, agreeing with листья. Perfective; желтеть describes the corresponding change of colour; future пожелтеют. It is intransitive and presents the colour change as a result. |
| ru-06-0058 | C2a | blushed; Masculine singular past, agreeing with Олег. Perfective; краснеть describes the corresponding change; future покраснеет. It is intransitive and presents the onset of visible redness. |
| ru-06-0059 | C2a | began to doze; Plural past, agreeing with пассажиры. Perfective ingressive; дремать describes the resulting activity; future задремлют. It is intransitive and marks the onset of dozing. |
| ru-06-0060 | C2a | began hissing; Feminine singular past, agreeing with кошка. Perfective ingressive; шипеть describes the corresponding sound; future зашипит. It is intransitive and marks the onset of hissing. |
| ru-06-0061 | C2a | cut up a quantity; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Даша. Perfective «наре́зать», future «наре́жет», presents the completed cutting of a quantity here. Imperfective «нареза́ть», present «нареза́ет», names the corresponding slicing activity without itself expressing accumulation. The genitive object expresses an indefinite quantity. Нарезать овощей means to cut up a quantity of vegetables; the genitive expresses an indefinite amount. |
| ru-06-0062 | C2a | chopped a quantity; Masculine singular past, agreeing with дедушка. Perfective; the activity base is колоть, and there is no simple imperfective partner for this accumulated-quantity use; future наколет. It takes a genitive quantity and presents an accumulated result. Наколоть дров means to chop a quantity of firewood. |
| ru-06-0063 | C2a | baked a quantity; Feminine singular past, agreeing with бабушка. Perfective; the activity base is печь, and there is no simple imperfective partner for this accumulated-quantity use; future напечёт. It takes a genitive quantity and presents the baked goods as an accumulation. |
| ru-06-0064 | C2a | took among themselves; Plural past, agreeing with дети. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner разбирать; future разберут. It takes an accusative object and presents the items as taken by different members of the group. Разобрать краски here means that the children took the paints among themselves. |
| ru-06-0065 | C2a | sorted into places; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Ольга. Perfective; imperfective partner раскладывать; future разложит. It takes an accusative object and по with the dative for the separate places. The form presents the distribution as complete. |
| ru-06-0066 | C2a | gave out; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Анна. Perfective; imperfective partner раздавать; future раздаст. It takes an accusative object and a dative recipient, presenting the items as distributed. |
| ru-06-0067 | C2a | placed in position; Plural past, agreeing with рабочие. Perfective; imperfective partner расставлять; future расставят. It takes an accusative object and presents the chairs as placed at separate positions. |
| ru-06-0068 | C2a | covered by sticking on; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Даша. Perfective; imperfective partner обклеивать. Future обкле́ит. It takes an accusative object and the instrumental for the material attached; the completed action covers the object's surface. Обклеить коробку звёздами means to cover the box with stars stuck onto it. |
| ru-06-0069 | C2a | tried on one after another; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Оля. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner перемеривать. Future переме́рит. It takes an accusative object and presents the trying on as distributed over several items. Перемерить платья here means to try on the dresses in turn, rather than to measure them again. |
| ru-06-0070 | C2a | carried and accumulated a quantity; Plural past, agreeing with белки. Perfective; the activity base is таскать, and there is no simple imperfective partner for this accumulated-quantity use; future натаскают. It takes a genitive quantity and в with the accusative for the destination. The base denotes repeated carrying, and на- marks accumulation. «Таскать» is an imperfective multidirectional carrying base; the means of transport is unspecified. |
| ru-06-0071 | C2a | warmed up; Feminine singular reflexive past, agreeing with вода. Perfective; imperfective partner нагреваться; future нагреется. It is intransitive and presents the stated temperature as reached. |
| ru-06-0072 | C2a | hardened; Masculine singular past, agreeing with клей. Perfective; imperfective partner твердеть; future затвердеет. It is intransitive and presents the glue as having reached a hard state. |
| ru-06-0073 | C2a | faded; Feminine singular past, agreeing with краска. Perfective; imperfective partner блекнуть; future поблекнет. It is intransitive and presents the loss of colour as an achieved change. P: Both «побле́кла» and «поблёкла» are normative variants; the sentence presents the colour loss by evening. |
| ru-06-0074 | C2a | finished painting; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Лена. Perfective; imperfective partner докрашивать; future докрасит. It takes an accusative object and presents the remaining painting as completed. Докрасить indicates completion of painting that had already begun. |
| ru-06-0075 | C2a | finished ironing; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Нина. Perfective; imperfective partner доглаживать. Future догла́дит. It takes an accusative object and presents the remaining ironing as completed before the shirt is put away. Догладить means to finish ironing something already partly ironed. |
| ru-06-0076 | C2a | added liquid; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Маша. Perfective; imperfective partner доливать; future дольёт. It takes an accusative object and в with the accusative for the container. Here the addition brings the kettle to the stated full level. Долить means to add liquid to what is already in a container. |
| ru-06-0077 | C2a | finished treating; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Ирина. Perfective; imperfective partner долечивать. Future доле́чит. It takes an accusative object and here a dative person; the treatment is presented as completed. Долечить зуб means to complete treatment of a tooth after treatment has already begun. |
| ru-06-0078 | C2a | shrank; Masculine singular past, agreeing with свитер. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner садиться; future сядет. It is intransitive and presents the garment's reduction in size as complete. With clothing after washing, сесть means to shrink; it does not describe sitting or movement. |
| ru-06-0079 | C2a | finished ripening; Plural past, agreeing with яблоки. Perfective; imperfective partner дозревать; future дозреют. It is intransitive and presents ripening as completed. Дозреть describes reaching ripeness after an earlier period of ripening. |
| ru-06-0080 | C2a | became icy; Plural past, agreeing with перчатки. Perfective; imperfective partner леденеть; future заледенеют. It is intransitive and presents the change to an icy state as achieved. |
| ru-06-0081 | C2a | breathed for a while; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Марина. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. Дышать is the activity base; future подышит. It takes the instrumental for what is breathed and presents the activity as a bounded episode. |
| ru-06-0082 | C2a | danced for a while; Plural past, agreeing with ученики. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. Танцевать is the activity base; future потанцуют. Used without an object, it presents dancing as a bounded activity. |
| ru-06-0083 | C2a | simmer gently for a while; Third-person singular reflexive future, agreeing with соус. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. Томиться is the activity base; future потомятся. It is intransitive; пусть introduces an instruction for a bounded period. With food, томиться describes slow cooking over gentle heat. |
| ru-06-0084 | C2a | smoked for a while; Masculine singular past, agreeing with Олег. Perfective delimitative; no simple imperfective partner. Курить is the activity base; future покурит. Used without an object, it presents a bounded smoking episode. |
| ru-06-0085 | C2a | began running about; Plural past, agreeing with прохожие. Perfective ingressive, based on imperfective multidirectional «бегать»; future «за́бегают». It is intransitive here and marks the start of running about on foot, without a single route. This prefixed onset contrasts with the directionality of the base. This ingressive form differs from imperfective забегать meaning to run in or drop by. |

| ru-06-0188 | C2b | sent; Feminine singular past, referring to Лена. Perfective; imperfective partner отправлять; future отправит. Takes an accusative object; the reported statement claims a completed sending. The office reports that it does not have the form; this does not settle whether it was sent. |
| ru-06-0189 | C2b | switched off; Feminine singular past, referring to Анна. Perfective; imperfective partner выключать; future выключит. Takes an accusative object; the attributed statement claims a completed switch-off. The later observation leaves open what happened between Anna's claimed action and Oleg's observation. |
| ru-06-0190 | C2b | returned; Masculine singular past, referring to Павел. Perfective; imperfective partner возвращать; future вернёт. Takes an accusative object; the reported statement claims a completed return. The library's current record gives reason to check the claim, but may itself be out of date. |
| ru-06-0191 | C2b | did not learn; Negated masculine singular past, agreeing with хор. Perfective; imperfective partner разучивать; future разучит. Takes an accusative object; negation denies that learning the finale was complete by the end of rehearsal. Разучить a piece means to learn it for performance. |
| ru-06-0192 | C2b | did not photograph; Negated feminine singular past, agreeing with Анна. Фотографировать is imperfective here and can also be perfective; сфотографировать is a contextual perfective for taking a photograph. Present фотографирует. Takes an accusative object; negation denies the activity during the tour. |
| ru-06-0193 | C2b | did not freeze; Negated feminine singular past, agreeing with вода. Perfective; imperfective partner замерзать; future замёрзнет. Intransitive; negation denies the change of state by morning. |
| ru-06-0194 | C2b | did not interrupt; Negated masculine singular past, agreeing with Игорь. Imperfective; perfective partner перебить; present перебивает. Takes an accusative person; negation covers the conversation until its end. |
| ru-06-0195 | C2b | cover; Perfective infinitive governed by не успели. Perfective; imperfective partner накрывать; future накроет. Takes an accusative object; the construction denies achieving the covering before the rain. |
| ru-06-0196 | C2b | did not sew; Negated feminine singular past, agreeing with Маша. Imperfective; сшить is a contextual perfective for making an item; present шьёт. Used without an object here; negation denies the activity during the week. |
| ru-06-0197 | C2b | did not bake; Negated feminine singular past, agreeing with Оля. Perfective; imperfective partner печь; future испечёт. Takes an accusative object; negation denies completion of the whole batch by opening time. Some rolls may have been baked; the statement concerns the full set. |
| ru-06-0198 | C2b | trust; Imperfective infinitive governed by не стоило. Imperfective «доверять»; present «доверяет». It takes the dative. For a particular act of believing the rumors, the contextual expression is perfective «поверить слухам», rather than an unqualified simple pairing with «довериться». The retrospective negative evaluation concerns trusting the rumors. |
| ru-06-0199 | C2b | spilled; Masculine singular past, agreeing with ребёнок. Perfective; imperfective partner проливать; future прольёт. Takes an accusative object; дважды counts two separate spills. |
| ru-06-0200 | C2b | did not reach agreement; Negated plural past with к plus dative; участники is the subject. Прийти is perfective; the contextual imperfective is приходить; future придёт. In this figurative construction it does not describe physical movement. Negation denies reaching agreement by evening. Прийти к согласию means to reach agreement; по цене specifies the issue. |
| ru-06-0201 | C2c | reduction; Genitive singular neuter after против. Возразить против names the proposed action opposed. |
| ru-06-0202 | C2c | results; Accusative plural inanimate after на. Сослаться на introduces evidence cited in support of a position. |
| ru-06-0203 | C2c | verification; Prepositional singular feminine after на. Настаивать на identifies the action the editor insists on. |
| ru-06-0204 | C2c | one's own position; Prepositional singular neuter after на; the pronoun stands for a position. Настоять на своём means to prevail in maintaining one's position. |
| ru-06-0205 | C2c | causes; Prepositional plural after о. Спорить о introduces the subject under dispute. |
| ru-06-0206 | C2c | position; Accusative singular feminine inanimate after на. Претендовать на identifies the position the candidates seek. |
| ru-06-0207 | C2c | Anna; Instrumental singular feminine animate after с. Поспорить с identifies the person with whom Pavel argued. |
| ru-06-0208 | C2c | reliability; Prepositional singular feminine after в. Усомниться в identifies the quality whose presence is questioned. |
| ru-06-0209 | C2c | data; Accusative plural inanimate after на. «Опираться на» identifies the data on which the researcher relies in the article. |
| ru-06-0210 | C2c | conclusions; Genitive plural after от. Предостеречь кого-либо от identifies the action or outcome one warns someone against. |
| ru-06-0211 | C2c | the fact; Prepositional singular neuter after в; the clause beginning with что specifies its content. Упрекнуть кого-либо в том, что introduces the conduct for which someone is reproached. |
| ru-06-0212 | C2c | tone; Accusative singular masculine inanimate after за. Упрекнуть кого-либо за identifies the specific grounds for reproach. |
| ru-06-0213 | C2c | procedure; Prepositional singular masculine after о. Договориться о names what the participants agreed upon. |
| ru-06-0214 | C2c | author; Dative singular masculine animate. Уступить кому-либо право identifies the person to whom a right is conceded. |
| ru-06-0215 | C2c | demands; Dative plural neuter. Уступить требованиям means to yield to demands. |
| ru-06-0216 | C2c | distortion; Prepositional singular neuter after в. Обвинить кого-либо в names the alleged wrongdoing; the accusation is attributed to the reviewer. |
| ru-06-0217 | C2c | reviewer; Dative singular masculine animate. Возразить кому-либо identifies the person addressed by an objection. |
| ru-06-0218 | C2c | discussion; Genitive singular neuter after от. Уклониться от identifies the discussion avoided. |
| ru-06-0219 | C2c | extension; Accusative singular neuter after за. Выступить за expresses public support for the proposed action. |
| ru-06-0220 | C2c | terms; Dative plural neuter. Противоречить takes the dative for what a statement or provision conflicts with. |
| ru-06-0221 | C2c | wishes; Dative plural neuter. Соответствовать takes the dative for the wishes with which the schedule accords. |
| ru-06-0222 | C2c | necessity; Prepositional singular feminine after в. Усомниться в необходимости questions whether something is needed. |
| ru-06-0223 | C2c | description; Instrumental singular neuter. Ограничиться takes the instrumental for what someone confines an activity to. |
| ru-06-0224 | C2c | opinions; Instrumental plural neuter. Обменяться мнениями names what the participants shared; с introduces the other party. |
| ru-06-0225 | C2c | caution; Dative singular feminine after к. Призвать кого-либо к introduces the conduct urged. |
| ru-06-0226 | C2c | compromise; Dative singular masculine after к. Прийти к компромиссу describes reaching a compromise; по срокам specifies the issue. |
| ru-06-0227 | C2c | right; Genitive singular neuter. Лишать кого-либо чего-либо identifies what someone is deprived of. The assessment is attributed to the lawyer. |
| ru-06-0228 | C2c | customer; Dative singular masculine animate. Обеспечить кому-либо доступ identifies the person given access. |
| ru-06-0229 | C2c | committee; Dative singular feminine. Поручить кому-либо подготовить identifies the body assigned the task. |
| ru-06-0230 | C2c | assessment; Genitive singular feminine after от. Воздержаться от identifies an action someone refrains from taking. |
| ru-06-0231 | C2c | data; Dative plural. Противопоставлять что чему places the accusative item in contrast with the dative item. |
| ru-06-0232 | C2c | shortage; Instrumental singular feminine. Объяснить что чем gives the cause offered for an event; the explanation is attributed to management. |
| ru-06-0233 | C2c | two; Genitive form of the numeral; it governs the genitive plural рабочих мест. Выделение governs the genitive of the quantity allocated. |
| ru-06-0234 | C2c | vote; Genitive singular masculine after одного. Не хватить governs the genitive for the quantity lacking. |
| ru-06-0235 | C2c | seven people; Nominative collective numeral, subject of «поддержали». Cardinal «семь» is also accepted with the omitted «участников». Из десяти участников identifies the larger group from which the seven are counted. |
| ru-06-0236 | C2c | with January; С plus instrumental singular masculine. По сравнению с introduces the reference period for the comparison. |
| ru-06-0237 | C2c | minutes; Dative singular masculine after согласно. Согласно attributes the statement to the meeting record. |
| ru-06-0238 | C2c | from the first version; От plus genitive singular feminine; первой agrees with версии. В отличие от introduces the item against which a difference is stated. |
| ru-06-0239 | C2c | Anna; Genitive singular feminine animate after за исключением. За исключением marks Anna as the exception to the stated support. |
| ru-06-0240 | C2c | to the report; К plus dative singular masculine. В дополнение к identifies the report to which the table was added. |
| ru-06-0241 | C2c | absence; Accusative singular neuter after за. Раскритиковать за names the grounds for criticism. |
| ru-06-0242 | C2c | argument; Prepositional singular masculine after на. Сосредоточиться на identifies the object of concentrated attention. |
| ru-06-0243 | C2c | conclusions; Genitive plural masculine after от. Отмежеваться от expresses a deliberate distancing from a position. |
| ru-06-0244 | C2c | references; Instrumental plural feminine. Подкрепить что чем identifies the support offered for a claim. |
| ru-06-0245 | C2c | interests; Dative plural masculine after к. Апеллировать к identifies the consideration invoked in an argument. |
| ru-06-0246 | C2c | absence; Genitive singular neuter after ввиду. Ввиду introduces the stated reason for the decision; it is typical of formal written explanations. |
| ru-06-0247 | C2c | dispute; Accusative singular masculine inanimate after на. Намёк на identifies what is alluded to indirectly. |
| ru-06-0248 | C2c | proof; Prepositional singular neuter after в. Пробел в identifies the argument or work containing a gap. |
| ru-06-0249 | C2c | parking; Genitive singular feminine after относительно. Относительно identifies the subject of the decision. |
| ru-06-0250 | C2c | reproach; Accusative singular masculine inanimate, matching the object of воспринял. «Воспринять что как что» identifies the perceiver’s interpretation of something. |
| ru-06-0251 | C2c | readiness; Accusative singular feminine after за. Поблагодарить за names the reason for thanks; the infinitive specifies what the colleagues were ready to do. |
| ru-06-0252 | C2c | change; Instrumental singular neuter after с. Связывать что с чем presents a relationship between two things. |
| ru-06-0253 | C2c | assumptions; Accusative plural neuter inanimate; object of разграничила. Разграничить joins the things distinguished as coordinated accusative objects. |
| ru-06-0254 | C2c | assessment; Instrumental singular feminine after между; coordinated with описанием. Проводить различие между identifies the things being distinguished. |
| ru-06-0255 | C2c | author; Genitive singular masculine animate after у. Запросить что у кого identifies the person from whom something is requested. |
| ru-06-0256 | C2c | quality; Dative singular neuter after к. Претензии к identifies what is the subject of complaints. |
| ru-06-0257 | C2c | reference; Instrumental singular feminine. Ответить на замечание чем identifies the content used as a response. |
| ru-06-0258 | C2c | words; Dative plural neuter after по. По словам attributes the statement to the person named in the genitive. |
| ru-06-0259 | C2c | doubt; Accusative singular neuter after под. Поставить под сомнение что means to question a claim or quality; точность is the thing questioned. |
| ru-06-0260 | C2c | assessment; Instrumental singular feminine after с. Несогласие с identifies the opinion or assessment rejected. |
| ru-06-0261 | C2c | supporters; Accusative plural masculine animate after на, coordinated with противников. Разделиться на identifies the groups into which people split. |
| ru-06-0262 | C2c | list; Genitive singular masculine after из. Исключить что из чего identifies the set from which items are removed. |
| ru-06-0263 | C2c | lawyer; Instrumental singular masculine animate after с. Согласовать что с кем identifies the person with whom something is agreed or cleared. |
| ru-06-0264 | C2c | parties; Accusative plural feminine after обе; object of устраивает. Устраивать кого means to be acceptable to someone. |
| ru-06-0265 | C2c | table; Instrumental singular feminine after с. Расхождение с identifies the reference with which something does not match. |
| ru-06-0266 | C2c | place; Accusative singular neuter; direct object of «уступили». Уступить место чему is used figuratively for giving way to something; the dative names what takes its place. |
| ru-06-0267 | C2c | unfounded; Instrumental singular feminine, agreeing with жалобу in gender and number. Признать что каким identifies the judgment made about something. |
| ru-06-0268 | C2c | one quarter; Accusative singular feminine after на. Превысить что на четверть states that the actual number exceeds the forecast by one quarter of the forecast. |
| ru-06-0269 | C2c | one third; Accusative singular feminine; object of одобрила. Из двадцати заявок supplies the whole of which the approved applications are a third. |
| ru-06-0270 | C2c | week; Genitive singular feminine after не более. Не более with the genitive sets an upper limit on the time allotted. |
| ru-06-0271 | C2c | half; Genitive singular feminine after около; сотрудников is also genitive plural. Около половины marks an approximate proportion of the group. |
| ru-06-0272 | C2c | by half; Сократить вдвое means to reduce something to half its former amount. |
| ru-06-0273 | C2c | twice; Вдвое больше expresses twice the quantity; заявок is genitive plural after больше. |
| ru-06-0274 | C2c | part; Accusative singular feminine; object of вернуть. Оплаты is genitive singular. Часть with the genitive identifies the whole from which an unspecified portion is taken. |
| ru-06-0275 | C2c | each; Genitive singular feminine, agreeing with «ошибки» after «для». P: «Для каждой исправленной ошибки» identifies each corrected error to which a page number corresponds. |
| ru-06-0276 | C2c | both; Genitive plural, agreeing with писем. Копии обоих писем specifies copies of each of the two letters. |
| ru-06-0277 | C2c | with comments; С plus instrumental plural neuter. Вместе с identifies the material accompanying the list. |
| ru-06-0278 | C2c | about the timing; О plus prepositional plural masculine. Речь шла о introduces the topic; исключительно limits the topic of the reply. |
| ru-06-0279 | C2c | request; Dative singular feminine after по. По просьбе identifies the request that prompted the action; автора identifies the requester. |
| ru-06-0280 | C2c | open; Instrumental singular masculine, agreeing in gender and number with вопрос. Оставить вопрос открытым means to leave a question unresolved. |
| ru-06-0281 | C2c | in the vote; При takes the prepositional singular голосовании. Воздержаться при голосовании identifies the vote in which someone abstains. |
| ru-06-0282 | C2c | publication; В takes the prepositional singular публикации. Отказать кому-либо в чём-либо identifies what is refused. |
| ru-06-0283 | C2c | publication; От takes the genitive singular публикации. Отказаться от чего-либо identifies an action or option one gives up. |
| ru-06-0284 | C2c | against a hasty decision; «От» governs genitive singular neuter «решения»; «поспешного» agrees with it. Предостеречь кого-либо от чего-либо names the action one warns someone against. |
| ru-06-0285 | C2c | a possible delay; О takes the prepositional singular; возможной agrees with задержке. Предупредить кого-либо о чём-либо introduces the event someone is warned about. |
| ru-06-0286 | C2c | about the noise; На takes the accusative singular masculine шум. Пожаловаться на identifies the cause of a complaint. |
| ru-06-0287 | C2c | to the administration; В takes the accusative singular feminine администрацию. Пожаловаться в identifies the institution receiving the complaint. |
| ru-06-0288 | C2c | the error; На takes the accusative singular feminine ошибку. Указать на ошибку means to point out an error. |
| ru-06-0289 | C2c | the response deadline; Срок is accusative singular masculine; ответа is genitive singular. Указать что means to state or specify information. |
| ru-06-0290 | C2c | with the organizers; С takes the instrumental plural организаторами. Согласовать что с кем identifies the people with whom a text is cleared. |
| ru-06-0291 | C2c | information; Genitive plural neuter after не хватало. Не хватать чего-либо identifies what is insufficient. |
| ru-06-0292 | C2c | against the sale; Против takes the genitive singular feminine продажи. Выступить против identifies a proposal or action opposed publicly. |
| ru-06-0293 | C2c | objections; Dative plural neuter after вопреки. Вопреки marks the objections as contrary to the decision. |
| ru-06-0294 | C2c | insufficient; Instrumental plural, matching доказательства in number. Счесть что каким expresses a judgment about the object. |
| ru-06-0295 | C2c | personal judgments; От takes the genitive plural; личных agrees with оценок. Воздержаться от identifies what someone is asked to avoid. |
| ru-06-0296 | C2c | completeness; Instrumental singular feminine. Отличаться чем identifies the feature that distinguishes the second version. |
| ru-06-0297 | C2c | notes; Instrumental plural neuter; подробными agrees with примечаниями. Снабдить что чем identifies the material added to a work; here the editor supplied the manuscript with notes. |
| ru-06-0298 | C2c | two days; Genitive numeral двух with genitive plural дней. Менее governs the genitive and sets an upper limit on the allotted time. |
| ru-06-0299 | C2c | one hundred employees; Genitive numeral ста with genitive plural сотрудников. Свыше sets a lower bound that the number exceeds. |
| ru-06-0300 | C2c | in addition to the rent; Помимо takes the genitive singular feminine аренды. «Помимо» marks the rent as an existing expense in addition to which the customer must pay for cleaning. |
| ru-06-0301 | C2d | get there; Perfective reflexive infinitive governed by можно. Perfective; imperfective добираться. Future доберусь, доберутся. До takes the genitive endpoint; the infinitive expresses a possible completed journey. До- marks reaching the destination, while the verb itself leaves the direction and means open. На пароме specifies the only available means of reaching the island. |
| ru-06-0302 | C2d | runs; Third-person singular present, agreeing with поезд. Imperfective; no simple perfective partner in this scheduled-route sense. First conjugation: следуют. До takes the genitive endpoint; the present describes the train's route. Следовать до is a formal way to describe a service running to a destination. |
| ru-06-0303 | C2d | turned; Past masculine singular, agreeing with автобус. Perfective; imperfective сворачивать. Future свернёт. С takes the genitive source and на the accusative new route; the past presents one completed turn by the bus. Свернуть с дороги на другую identifies both the route left and the route entered. |
| ru-06-0304 | C2d | passed; Past masculine singular, agreeing with поезд. Perfective in this completed passage; imperfective миновать is also used for repeated passage. Future минует. The accusative тоннель names what the train passed along its route. Миновать describes passing a place without specifying a particular direction of entry or exit. |
| ru-06-0305 | C2d | runs; Third-person singular present, agreeing with паром. Imperfective; no simple perfective partner for a continuing service. First conjugation: курсируют. Между takes the instrumental endpoints; the present describes repeated ferry travel. Курсировать между identifies the places linked by a regular service. |
| ru-06-0306 | C2d | forks; Third-person singular present reflexive, agreeing with тропа. Imperfective; perfective раздвоиться. First conjugation: раздваиваются. Раз- marks division into two branches; the present describes the path's layout. A path can раздваиваться where its route divides. |
| ru-06-0307 | C2d | turns; Third-person singular present, agreeing with маршрут. Imperfective; perfective свернуть. First conjugation: сворачивают. К takes the dative direction; the present describes the bus route's turn. К реке identifies the direction of the turn; the contrast excludes the market. |
| ru-06-0308 | C2d | shut down; Past feminine singular, agreeing with компания. Perfective; imperfective сворачивать. Future свернёт. The verb takes accusative программу; the past presents the shutdown as completed. С- contributes a lexicalized sense of bringing an activity to an end. Свернуть программу means to discontinue a program; no physical turn is implied. |
| ru-06-0309 | C2d | plotted; Past plural, agreeing with мы. Perfective; imperfective прокладывать. Future проложим, проложат. The verb takes accusative маршрут; the past presents the route as established. Про- contributes the sense of creating a continuous route through a space. Проложить маршрут means to plan or establish a route. Через лес gives its course, and в обход моста identifies what it avoids. |
| ru-06-0310 | C2d | got off; Past plural reflexive, agreeing with мы. Perfective; imperfective высаживаться. Future высадимся. Вы- marks completed departure from a vehicle; на станции identifies the place. Раньше конечной indicates that the passengers got off before the terminus. |
| ru-06-0311 | C2d | set off abruptly; Past plural reflexive, agreeing with бегуны. Perfective; contextual imperfective срываться. Future сорвутся. С takes the genitive in с места; со- marks a sudden departure from the starting point. The past presents the start as completed. Сорваться с места emphasizes an abrupt start; побежали makes the runners' movement toward the finish explicit. |
| ru-06-0312 | C2d | came alongside; Past feminine singular, agreeing with лодка. Perfective; imperfective причаливать. Future причалит. К takes the dative destination; the past presents the boat's completed arrival alongside the island. Причалить к берегу or острову describes bringing a vessel alongside land. |
| ru-06-0313 | C2d | pulled away; Masculine singular past, agreeing with «паром». Perfective «отойти»; imperfective «отходить». Compare future «отойдёт». The prefix marks departure from the genitive source after «от»; the ferry moves by water. The unprefixed directional base is «идти», versus multidirectional «ходить». P: «Отойти от пристани» describes a vessel leaving its berth. |
| ru-06-0314 | C2d | developed; Past neuter singular, agreeing with обсуждение. Perfective; imperfective перерастать. Future перерастёт. В takes the accusative resulting form; the past presents a completed change in the discussion. Перерасти в describes one activity or situation developing into another. |
| ru-06-0315 | C2d | returned; Past plural with an unspecified human subject. Perfective; imperfective возвращать. Future вернут. The verb takes accusative проект and dative автору; на доработку states the purpose of the completed return. Вернуть документ на доработку means to return it for further revision. |
| ru-06-0316 | C2d | was received; Past masculine singular, agreeing with план. Perfective; imperfective поступать. Future поступит. В takes the accusative receiving department; the past presents receipt as completed. A document can поступить в отдел when the department receives it. |
| ru-06-0317 | C2d | sent; Past feminine singular, agreeing with редакция. Perfective; imperfective направлять. Future направит. The verb takes accusative запрос and в with the accusative recipient institution; the past presents the dispatch as completed. Направить запрос is a formal way to say that a request was sent. |
| ru-06-0318 | C2d | spread; Past feminine singular reflexive, agreeing with новость. Perfective; imperfective распространяться. Future распространится. Среди takes the genitive group; the past presents dissemination as completed. Распространиться среди сотрудников describes information becoming known within that group. |
| ru-06-0319 | C2d | withdrew; Past neuter singular, agreeing with издательство. Perfective; imperfective отзывать. Future отзовёт, отзовут. The verb takes accusative книгу and из with the genitive; ото- expresses withdrawal in this lexicalized use. The past presents the action as completed. Отозвать книгу из продажи means to withdraw it from sale. |
| ru-06-0320 | C2d | died down; Past masculine singular, agreeing with спор. Perfective; imperfective утихать. Future утихнет. The intransitive verb presents the dispute's gradual subsiding as a completed change. Спор утих means that the dispute became quieter; it does not assert that the issue was resolved. |
| ru-06-0321 | C2d | arrived; Past masculine singular, agreeing with поезд. Perfective; imperfective прибывать. Future прибудет. На plus accusative names the destination; the past presents arrival as completed. The verb does not itself specify a means of travel. Прибыть is common in formal travel announcements. |
| ru-06-0322 | C2d | boarding; Accusative singular feminine, the direct object of объявили. Посадка на рейс refers to boarding a flight; на takes the accusative. |
| ru-06-0323 | C2d | landed; Past masculine singular совершил agrees with самолёт; посадку is accusative singular. Perfective; imperfective совершать посадку. Future совершит посадку. На plus prepositional identifies the landing site; the past presents a completed landing. Совершить посадку is a formal expression used of an aircraft landing. |
| ru-06-0324 | C2d | changes direction; Third-person singular present меняет agrees with поезд; направление is accusative singular. Imperfective менять; perfective сменить in this frame. Меняет is first-conjugation; plural меняют. The accusative names the direction changed, and the present describes the train's route.
